Title: Seven Sermons upon the following subjects; viz. The difference betwixt truth and falshood, right and wrong; the natural abilities of men for discerning these differences; the right and duty of private judgment; objections considered; the love of God; the love of our neighbour; the first and great commandment, &c.; preached at a lecture in the West Meeting-House in Boston, begun the first Thursday in June, and ended the last Thursday in August, 1748
Creator: Jonathan Mayhew, A.M., Pastor of the West Church in Boston
Publisher: Boston, N.E.: printed and sold by Rogers and Fowle in Queen-street
Publication Date: 1749
Rights: Public Domain (published 1749, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Source: Internet Archive item sevensermonsupon00mayh (https://archive.org/details/sevensermonsupon00mayh), the item's own sevensermonsupon00mayh_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1749. Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary scan; good OCR, long s as f. A printed slip headed 'Elegant Extract' bound or pasted in before the sermons comes through in the OCR and is not part of the book.
Content note: The West Church minister's lectures on reason, conscience and the right of private judgment against creeds and implicit faith; an early public statement of the Boston liberal theology.
